# AI Media Scout — 2026-11-05

Expanded discovery adds underwater-media research and detailed source-backed guides for editing, subtitles, symbolic music, AI design, audio, 3D, motion and interactive workflows. Today’s 45 complete guides include earlier screened entries and one substantive ComfyUI v0.44 update; six additional eligible discoveries remain screened leads. Full software grants are reviewed separately from models, assets and hosted services. No discovered application was installed or run. All platforms are equally eligible, with actual documented support and unknown requirements stated per route. The weekly review of the existing 1,587-tool library was already complete; today’s new assessments address all six checks without implying recommendation or hands-on testing. Verified collection: 718/718 repository queries attempted; 718 successful, 0 partial, 0 failed and 123 bounded/truncated. All 18 model tasks were attempted; 0 failed. These are bounded discovery attempts, not an exhaustive census or quality approval. Separate from successful search queries, 29 watchlist fetches and 3 enrichment attempts failed; the detailed warnings remain in the report. A 404/409 is an availability gap, not proof of abandonment or license revocation.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

These primary-source observations are research context; they do not establish a new eligible tool, independent quality or a complete installation/license chain. [Source 1](https://invoke.ai/) |
| Video, animation & film | The current OpenReelio primary site was compared with the earlier snapshot. New guides cover reviewed source routes for Palmier, CutVoke, Cropus, Vision Studio and AI Video Studio, separating local rendering, remote model calls and owner-only acceptance tests. Palmier is scoped to its last GPL source tag, not the later proprietary binary. [Source 1](https://openreelio.com/) |

[Source 1](https://solidcraft.io/) |

## Search and review counts

86 creative fields; 718/718 repository queries attempted; 18/18 model-task queries attempted. 29888 distinct source candidates and 861 model leads. 45 detailed profiles and 6 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 0 failed repository queries, 0 partial repository queries, 123 bounded repository queries, 0 failed model-task queries and 2 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · searched: Fresh full primary documentation, actual source headers/tree and the referenced full GPLv3 terms support the new perso-ai symbolic-music guide. Embedded Amp Model, AudioStellar and LibreTerrain primary snapshots were also read/compared. Python 3.5-era instructions, MusicXML output and absent current environment lock are disclosed; a README/source grant is not invented as a standalone LICENSE file. [Source](https://gitlab.com/algomus.fr/perso-ai) [Source](https://gitlab.com/algomus.fr/perso-ai/-/blob/master/factor-oracle/melodygen.py) [Source](https://gitlab.com/ar39907/embedded-amp-model/-/raw/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/ayrsd/audiostellar/-/raw/audiostellar2/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/libreterrain/libreterrain/-/raw/main/README.md)
- **codeberg** · gap: The current Imaginer discovery attempt was denied by robots restrictions. No alternate route was used to bypass that restriction. This is a dated access gap, not evidence that the ecosystem lacks creative AI projects.
- **sourcehut** · gap: The attempted Diffuser source could not be opened through the live web tool. No usable current primary page was returned. Historical sources remain historical; this attempt does not establish project disappearance or a newly qualified tool.
- **packages** · searched: The current NuGet TensorStack.Media version index was read alongside source documentation. A published package version alone does not establish an independently reproducible inference/media workflow, so the earlier SDK lead remains pending deeper setup/API review. [Source](https://api.nuget.org/v3-flatcontainer/tensorstack.media/index.json) [Source](https://github.com/saddam213/TensorStack)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Current source reviews cover Meshroom/mrSegmentation prompt masks, Blender reconstruction integration, DCC Kenney assets, code-agent video tools and DJ MCP. Host/plugin/model rights remain separate. Previously completed canonical aliases are not recycled as new tools. [Source](https://github.com/alicevision/Meshroom) [Source](https://github.com/meshroomHub/mrSegmentation) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender) [Source](https://github.com/dcc-mcp/dcc-asset-kenney) [Source](https://github.com/Igriscodes/dj-mcp)
- **project-sites** · searched: All 86 observed fields have primary-source context attempts beyond repository discovery through 87 unique configured URLs: 86 HTTP successes and one OpenSlides SSL failure. Three raw PDF captures were bounded; additional live paper reads supplement their text. Unchanged source bytes, limited shells and page extraction changes are not claimed as new software. Extra targeted sources and failed web-tool attempts are archived separately. [Source](https://developers.meta.com/vr/blog/accelerate-vr-development-with-ai-and-immersive-web-sdk/) [Source](https://beta.nasjonalmuseet.no/2026/26/semantic-search-in-the-collection-using-local-ai/) [Source](https://zkm.de/en/open-source-code) [Source](https://linkedculture.org/about) [Source](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)
- **research-code** · searched: Underwater models, learned attribution, motion transfer and neural photogrammetry plugins extend this edition. Full grants were read. MagicDance and Bailando have research/non-commercial software restrictions and are excluded; a complete UniAnimate license was not returned. Paper results and GitHub SPDX badges do not override actual grants. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB) [Source](https://github.com/tencent/MimicMotion) [Source](https://github.com/Boese0601/MagicDance) [Source](https://github.com/lisiyao21/Bailando) [Source](https://github.com/ali-vilab/UniAnimate)
- **international** · searched: Current Chinese, English and other-language source documentation was reviewed across audio, design, video and 3D workflows, including Qwen Audio Agent, Video AI Voiceover, SVG Studio and Motion Edit. Source language, origin and stars do not establish output quality or model rights. ## UDnet V1 / V2

Neural underwater photography, film and scene restoration · Photography, restoration & color · Images & design · Video, animation & film

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Compare an original dive photograph or short underwater clip with a conservative V1/V2 restoration before any optional detail synthesis.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The owner gallery compares V1, V2 and an experimental AI Max branch on 24 scenes. The README reports model-only 256-pixel timing on RTX 4090/24-thread CPU; those figures are not full-resolution throughput or independent quality approval. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Clone the source, install requirements and use Inference.py with a small image folder or video. Released weights download on first use; the separate ai-max branch needs its own environment and is not part of the main quick start. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: README: remove the V1 score table. Latest sampled release: v2.0-preview. Current bounded support sample: Some issues (closed); I hope to be able to process 1920×1080 videos in real time (closed); How to reproduce on Windows system? (closed). At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/commit/d6b196bfef05b91eca0a05925686276a85af5f6c) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/releases/tag/v2.0-preview) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/issues/2) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/issues/5) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/issues/4)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Current documentation, implementation references and the bounded issue/PR sample were examined. Independent successful reproduction of the entire selected workflow remains unverified; this does not assert there are no independent users.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Complete MIT software grant reviewed. Released UDnet checkpoints, training datasets, inherited modules and optional Real-ESRGAN/DiffBIR assets have separate provenance/terms. The current README says intended for academic research, rather than the older indexed academic-only wording; no extra restriction appears in the reviewed MIT grant. Local compute/model storage; no required paid inference service is documented for the main route. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The released V1/V2 workflow is separate from the experimental ai-max diffusion branch. V2 can impose the appearance of its paired training references; optional restoration can synthesize uncaptured detail and is unsuitable for measurement. Video is processed frame by frame without a documented temporal-consistency guarantee and loses its audio track.

### How it uses AI

A PyTorch restoration pipeline uses learned underwater colour/contrast correction for images and individual video frames. V1 is the unsupervised paper model; released V2 learns from paired reference images. [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

### Introduction

A PyTorch restoration pipeline uses learned underwater colour/contrast correction for images and individual video frames. V1 is the unsupervised paper model; released V2 learns from paired reference images. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

### What it is good for

Compare an original dive photograph or short underwater clip with a conservative V1/V2 restoration before any optional detail synthesis. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

### Demo & examples

The owner gallery compares V1, V2 and an experimental AI Max branch on 24 scenes. The README reports model-only 256-pixel timing on RTX 4090/24-thread CPU; those figures are not full-resolution throughput or independent quality approval. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

### Install

Clone the source, install requirements and use Inference.py with a small image folder or video. Released weights download on first use; the separate ai-max branch needs its own environment and is not part of the main quick start. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the source, install requirements and use Inference.py with a small image folder or video. Released weights download on first use; the separate ai-max branch needs its own environment and is not part of the main quick start.
3. Commands below are source examples for the chosen route, not a cross-platform one-paste installer. Work inside the cloned repository and activate its environment before dependency commands; obtain the documented models/assets first when required.

```sh
git clone https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet.git
```


```sh
cd UDnet
```


```sh
pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
python Inference.py --input path/to/images --output results/ --model v1
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

1. Keep the original image or short clip and start with V1.
2. Compare V2 with the same input, checking colour and fine structure.
3. If testing x2/x4 detail synthesis, label invented texture and compare against the original.
4. Review the exported MP4 frame timing and restore an authorized audio track separately: the current video route does not copy audio.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

- **Hardware:** Main-route minimum RAM/VRAM/total disk is not documented. V1/V2 weight files are documented as about 5.6 MB/26 MB, not complete installation sizes. Only the separate AI Max branch states roughly 15 GB disk and about 10 GB GPU memory.
- **Software:** requirements.txt lists torch>=1.10, torchvision>=0.11, scikit-image>=0.19, NumPy, Pillow, OpenCV, SciPy and tqdm. A minimum Python version for the current main quick start is not specified.
- **Platforms:** CUDA is selected when available and CPU fallback is documented. A tested current host-OS matrix or Apple MPS route is not established.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 4](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** Complete MIT software grant reviewed. Released UDnet checkpoints, training datasets, inherited modules and optional Real-ESRGAN/DiffBIR assets have separate provenance/terms. The current README says intended for academic research, rather than the older indexed academic-only wording; no extra restriction appears in the reviewed MIT grant.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Complete MIT software grant reviewed. Released UDnet checkpoints, training datasets, inherited modules and optional Real-ESRGAN/DiffBIR assets have separate provenance/terms. The current README says intended for academic research, rather than the older indexed academic-only wording; no extra restriction appears in the reviewed MIT grant.
- **Cost:** Local compute/model storage; no required paid inference service is documented for the main route.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

### Limitations

The released V1/V2 workflow is separate from the experimental ai-max diffusion branch. V2 can impose the appearance of its paired training references; optional restoration can synthesize uncaptured detail and is unsuitable for measurement. Video is processed frame by frame without a documented temporal-consistency guarantee and loses its audio track. [Source 1](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/alzayats/UDnet/blob/main/requirements.txt) [Source 3](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/)

## UVE-Net / UVEB underwater video restoration

Neural underwater photography, film and scene restoration · Video, animation & film · Photography, restoration & color

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Research restoration of extracted underwater frame sequences while comparing colour, visibility and temporal artifacts with the source footage.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The paper/repository show UVEB, UIEB and CUDIE comparison figures. Owner benchmark figures do not establish performance on a new camera, water condition or delivery codec. [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the documented Ubuntu/PyTorch environment and work inside UVE-Net for its requirements, setup.py and basicsr scripts. Edit the supplied test YAML for your frame folders, compatible checkpoint and device count; its original absolute author paths are not portable. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: Update setup.py. Current bounded support sample: About whether to allow retransmission (closed); Terrible issue of the test dataset (open); Dataset Issue (open). At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. Current support reports black-frame inputs causing invalid UIQM arithmetic and a broken dataset-download link; no fix for every dataset/test configuration was established. [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/commit/a9c38b284aa9fca609b443c1857ecd8468ce7a7d) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/issues/14) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/issues/22) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/issues/21) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/issues/22) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/issues/20)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Current documentation, implementation references and the bounded issue/PR sample were examined. Independent successful reproduction of the entire selected workflow remains unverified; this does not assert there are no independent users.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Complete MIT source grant reviewed. Checkpoint and dataset redistribution/use terms remain separate from the code license. Local training/inference resources and separately governed dataset/checkpoint storage. [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The large/small model code requires the matching architecture and checkpoint. The test file retains GOPRO names and absolute paths despite this underwater workflow. A source comment about CPU mode is not a verified portable CPU installation; measurements and physical colour fidelity require separate validation. Current support reports black-frame inputs causing invalid UIQM arithmetic and a broken dataset-download link; no fix for every dataset/test configuration was established.

### How it uses AI

A PyTorch video restoration model exchanges information between adjacent underwater frames using frame-conditioned convolutional kernels; the repository supplies a benchmark, model code and a test pipeline. [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)

### Introduction

A PyTorch video restoration model exchanges information between adjacent underwater frames using frame-conditioned convolutional kernels; the repository supplies a benchmark, model code and a test pipeline. [Source 1](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source 3](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source 4](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)

### What it is good for

Research restoration of extracted underwater frame sequences while comparing colour, visibility and temporal artifacts with the source footage. [Source 1](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source 3](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source 4](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)

The paper/repository show UVEB, UIEB and CUDIE comparison figures. Owner benchmark figures do not establish performance on a new camera, water condition or delivery codec. ### Install

Use the documented Ubuntu/PyTorch environment and work inside UVE-Net for its requirements, setup.py and basicsr scripts. Edit the supplied test YAML for your frame folders, compatible checkpoint and device count; its original absolute author paths are not portable. [Source 1](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source 3](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source 4](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)

3. Commands below are source examples for the chosen route, not a cross-platform one-paste installer. Work inside the cloned repository and activate its environment before dependency commands; obtain the documented models/assets first when required.

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/options/test/Deblur/test_Deblur_GOPRO.yml) [Source 3](https://github.com/yzbouc/UVEB/blob/main/UVE-Net/requirements.txt) [Source 4](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/papers/Xie_UVEB_A_Large-scale_Benchmark_and_Baseline_Towards_Real-World_Underwater_Video_CVPR_2024_paper.pdf)

1. Prepare a short sequence as image frames using the documented folder organization.

3. Set paths and a compatible model explicitly before a controlled comparison.
4. Inspect saved frames and assemble/retain audio with a separate authorized editing workflow; a turnkey MP4/audio round trip is not documented.

### How it uses AI

```sh
python -m pip install -e .
```


```sh
uwir-train --model unet_5ch --dataset euvp --data_train_euvp ./datasets/EUVP --nEpochs 100 --batchSize 4 --cropSize 256 --seed 0
```


```sh
uwir-evaluate --eval_benchmark euvp --data_train_euvp ./datasets/EUVP --checkpoint_dir ./checkpoints --val_folder ./results/euvp
```

2. Prepare permitted paired data and preserve a held-out evaluation set.
3. Train the selected configuration and retain seeds/settings/checkpoints.
4. Inspect restoration errors and colour changes alongside PSNR/SSIM/CIEDE2000/UCIQE/UIQM; metrics alone do not establish natural-looking or physically faithful output.

v0.44.0 was published November 4 UTC (November 3 Eastern), after this library’s v0.43 guide. The cited H3 latent upscaler/VAE/context-window and ProRes/chroma export additions are the concrete creator-relevant changes; this is not a claim that the release happened today.

Current independent reproduction, complete selected-model terms and output reliability for the new route remain unverified.

## Vectopen — Linux AI image preparation

Vector graphics, illustration & textures · Images & design · Photography, restoration & color

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Linux 0.2.0-alpha packaged AI preprocessing and vector-editing workflow.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The current released package has a documented theme-change bug that can repaint selected objects and lose gradients; its fix is not yet released. Safe atomic save and damaged-page warnings remain unfinished in the next-release list. These are concrete current editing/save risks, rather than the alpha tag or untested Mac port. https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/ROADMAP.md

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Owner interface screenshots and a 0.2.0-alpha release are provided. The AI preprocessing bundle is specifically documented for Linux; Mac packages are explicitly not tested on a real Mac. [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** For the selected AI workflow use the official Linux package containing the native vectorizer/models. Contributors can open the source in standard Godot 4.7+, but Rust GDExtensions and model packaging are additional build concerns. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: chore(stats): descargas 2026-11-04. Latest sampled release: v0.2.2-beta. Current bounded support sample: Instaladores firmados, AppImage y prueba en Mac real (N-10) (open); Vectorize + IA en Windows y macOS (ONNX Runtime) (N-9) (open); Empaquetado: icono tiled, LICENSE y notices en todos los paquetes, RPM con licencia MIT (N-8) (open). At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/commit/aa177da9523770f18e192ac1fb959c3329dc5330) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/releases/tag/v0.2.2-beta) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/issues/9) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/issues/8) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/issues/7)

- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Complete MIT app grant reviewed. Godot/VTracer/ONNX Runtime, models, fonts and other Rust libraries retain their own notices; exact third-party distribution scope is separate. Local desktop compute and packaged model/native-library storage; no required paid model API is documented. [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. VTracer vectorization is not itself proof of learned inference. Owner large-document load times and 750+ unit tests are not independent performance/output evidence. A prerelease label and an untested Mac package do not alone prove the selected Linux core is unstable.

### How it uses AI

A Godot/Rust vector editor combines conventional VTracer vectorization with optional ONNX U²-Net background removal and Real-ESRGAN x4 preprocessing; an MCP surface can also expose undoable editor operations to an agent. [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/malabo1990/-Vectopen/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt)

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

Reported low-memory playback measurements are scoped CPU/container/Pi experiments; they do not establish memory needs for a 32k-context local LLM. [Source](https://github.com/OnkelTheorie/schneidi/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the Linux AppImage or matching Debian package, open a copied project and configure the chosen AI provider. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/OnkelTheorie/schneidi/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: test_game43: the picture shape box check compares with the box's size hint, not a fixed 30 px for arrow and frame. Latest sampled release: v0.16.1. The current issue/PR endpoint returned no records; this is not evidence of independent support or absence of defects. Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. This is not a universal semantic highlight detector: the described thresholds and strict false-positive/re-verification logic target a particular sound class. CPU detection does not guarantee that every configured encoder works without NVIDIA. Pause is not a disk-persistent compilation resume; remote validation can be skipped for quiet/repetitive audio. The experimental Any URL feature does not label the normal local-file pipeline Experimental.

### How it uses AI

A Windows clipping/compilation app runs an ONNX audio-event classifier, proposes time ranges and uses FFmpeg to review/cut/merge selected moments; the documented re-verification path is specifically tuned for burp detection. [Source](https://github.com/Vincent9295/Autocomper/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Windows clipping/compilation app runs an ONNX audio-event classifier, proposes time ranges and uses FFmpeg to review/cut/merge selected moments; the documented re-verification path is specifically tuned for burp detection. [Source 1](https://github.com/Vincent9295/Autocomper/blob/main/README.md)

### How it uses AI

A Python core captures one camera frame, obtains a bounded description from a local Qwen vision server, then sends validated text and optional prior generated artwork to a remote image API to build a fictional portrait lineage. 2. Use Python >=3.11 in an isolated environment, install the package and separately obtain an OpenCV camera, llama-server plus compatible Qwen model/projector, and authorized OpenAI Images access. Run doctor before any capture or paid call.
3. Commands below are source examples for the chosen route, not a cross-platform one-paste installer. Work inside the cloned repository and activate its environment before dependency commands; obtain the documented models/assets first when required.

```sh
python -m venv .venv
```


```sh
python -m pip install -e .
```


```sh
python -m portrait_seed doctor
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/PRIVACY.md)

1. Prepare the local loopback vision service and privately configured API access.
2. With participant authorization, use the separate describe stage to inspect a camera-derived text job before submitting it.
3. Review the remote image-generation boundary and the up-to-three-total-attempt budget before enabling a paid request.
4. Inspect/export the saved generated PNG and metadata; the collection persists in SQLite as well as exported files.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/PRIVACY.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python >=3.11, OpenCV-compatible camera, separate local llama-server with matching multimodal model/projector, and an authorized image API account. Model files and keys are not included.
- **Platforms:** Windows and Linux setup instructions are documented; other host/device routes are not established. A local camera and local model server are required for the described capture path.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/PRIVACY.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** Complete MIT code grant reviewed. Qwen/GGUF/projector licenses, local server dependencies and the required hosted image API/output terms remain separate.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Complete MIT code grant reviewed. Qwen/GGUF/projector licenses, local server dependencies and the required hosted image API/output terms remain separate.
- **Cost:** Local vision-model compute/storage plus paid image API requests; no current price or account availability was verified.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/PRIVACY.md)

### Limitations

Descriptions can contain personal information and leave the device, as does a previous generated reference image. The package does not intentionally save raw camera frames, but that is not an OS/server privacy certification. Completed descriptions/images have no automatic expiry. It is not face-recognition memory or a complete installation renderer, and a lost paid response can consume a billed attempt. [Source 1](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/sidecube/portrait-seed/blob/main/docs/PRIVACY.md)

## PuppetControl

Neural rigging and digital puppetry · AI cel animation and inbetweening · Video, animation & film · AI agents for code-authored media production

The repository was created on 2026-11-05. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Current prototype supports more than the initial silent single-pose sample, but smooth bridges, automatic rig creation and broader composition remain unfinished. Experimental is supported by those current scoped limitations.

Scope: Agent-authored JSON scenes for prepared Rev2D characters, including the documented small single-pose and hard-cut multipose previews; optional one-track Rhubarb audio is assessed separately.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The current README calls the renderer experimental/runnable and shows a hard-cut prototype; smooth pose bridges, automatic rig/AI-pose generation, multi-actor composition and camera composition remain unfinished. New audio alignment does not resolve those software-stage limitations. https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The original single-pose scene remains four seconds at 512×640/15 fps. The current README adds a silent 512×640 three-pose hard-cut demo. The audio guide reports one local Windows 8.533-second, 128-frame/15-fps AAC voiceover example; its voice recording is not committed. These owner examples do not establish general lip-sync or multi-pose/audio quality. [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use Node >=22, Git and FFmpeg; install locked dependencies, explicitly prepare the pinned Rev2D checkout and build the CLI. Optional alignment needs a separately installed Rhubarb executable and WAV speech; no built-in Rhubarb download, transcription or TTS provider is supplied. Independent reproduction of these current routes has not been established. [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: feat: add agent-driven Rev2D scene preview and export. The current issue/PR endpoint returned no records; this is not evidence of independent support or absence of defects. At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. The rechecked source revision and updated documentation were read during final alignment; current claims remain documented, not independently reproduced. [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/commit/a7b704d9ccbdc49cde0dfba9c11cb6a47fbf062b) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/puppetcontrol/issues) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/commit/fe58b325417e3a320cef76c20c4b563cb1a72d47)

Rev2D’s Hana rig is obtained from a separately pinned MIT upstream; third-party art, voice assets and FFmpeg builds retain separate terms. Optional Rhubarb and user-supplied speech/voices add their own notices and terms; no built-in TTS provider or automatic aligner download is supplied. [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Imported-art auto-rigging, AI-generated pose rigs, genuinely smooth between-pose bridges, automatic transcription/TTS, captions, multiple actors, a camera compositor and 1080p sample output remain unfinished. Three-pose rendering uses visible hard cuts. The audio guide still restricts its renderer to one actor/rig family; it does not prove combined multipose/audio behavior. Rhubarb is mainly English-focused and is not installed implicitly.

### How it uses AI

An external coding agent authors JSON character scenes for a pinned Rev2D rig. Current source documents frame/MP4 export, hard-cut switches between separately prepared pose rigs and optional single-track audio/Rhubarb mouth cues; it does not generate every frame with an image model. [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)

### Introduction

An external coding agent authors JSON character scenes for a pinned Rev2D rig. Current source documents frame/MP4 export, hard-cut switches between separately prepared pose rigs and optional single-track audio/Rhubarb mouth cues; it does not generate every frame with an image model. [Source 1](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)

Explore reusable-character animation by asking a coding agent to author a small scene within the sample rig’s declared capabilities. [Source 1](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The original single-pose scene remains four seconds at 512×640/15 fps. The current README adds a silent 512×640 three-pose hard-cut demo. The audio guide reports one local Windows 8.533-second, 128-frame/15-fps AAC voiceover example; its voice recording is not committed. These owner examples do not establish general lip-sync or multi-pose/audio quality. ### Install

Use Node >=22, Git and FFmpeg; install locked dependencies, explicitly prepare the pinned Rev2D checkout and build the CLI. Optional alignment needs a separately installed Rhubarb executable and WAV speech; no built-in Rhubarb download, transcription or TTS provider is supplied. [Source 1](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)

1. Clone the official source and prepare Node >=22, Git and FFmpeg.
2. Install locked dependencies, prepare the pinned Rev2D rig and run the documented verification/build command.
3. Start with the original single-pose sample. Optional three-pose generation and Rhubarb audio alignment are separate documented routes.
4. Install Rhubarb separately only if using alignment; align WAV speech and supply its cues plus a supported audio track in the scene JSON.

```sh
npm ci
```


```sh
npm run rig:setup
```


```sh
npm run rig:status
```


```sh

```





```sh

```


### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/docs/AUDIO_WORKFLOW.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/nkcbuilds/PuppetControl/blob/main/package.json)

1. Copy a supported scene and have the agent edit declared head/gaze/expression events.
2. Validate and inspect its frame preview before exporting.
3. For multiple prepared pose families, use explicit hard-cut boundaries; the source does not provide smooth posture transitions.
4. For optional audio, generate Rhubarb cues separately and reference them with one WAV/MP3 track; inspect starts, pauses and ends for alignment.
5. Keep the audio guide’s one-actor/rig-family restriction distinct from the new silent multipose demonstration; joint audio/multipose acceptance is not established.

### How it uses AI

A Python SRT translator batches subtitle text through a local Ollama TranslateGemma model, using preceding dialogue as context while retaining the source cue indices/timestamps. [Source](https://github.com/erastodib/SubForge/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Python SRT translator batches subtitle text through a local Ollama TranslateGemma model, using preceding dialogue as context while retaining the source cue indices/timestamps. [Source 1](https://github.com/erastodib/SubForge/blob/main/README.md)

Source review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this application. No per-user separation is supplied in the no-login LAN default; access depends on the permitted private network and possession of a job ID. It is not a public Internet deployment. The current source says the new LAN mode still needs CI and actual-server validation. DOCX lacks phrase timestamps; bilingual/dialect accuracy is not guaranteed. Initial model download and YouTube require network access, while local-file inference is a separate route. Backups/models persist beyond automatic job cleanup.

### How it uses AI

A FastAPI private-LAN web app runs local Faster-Whisper jobs from uploaded media or permitted YouTube sources, supports transcript editing and creates editable DOCX. The current default has no user login and depends on access to the assigned private server network. [Source](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

### Introduction

A FastAPI private-LAN web app runs local Faster-Whisper jobs from uploaded media or permitted YouTube sources, supports transcript editing and creates editable DOCX. The current default has no user login and depends on access to the assigned private server network. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

### What it is good for

Draft Spanish/Catalan interview transcripts on an Ubuntu CPU server for browser users on a controlled private LAN. Review the text before exporting Word; the current LAN route does not require a desktop GUI or an SSH tunnel for each user. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

### Demo & examples

The updated test report separates prior tiny-model silence inference and CPU Docker/HTTP checks from 27 current local LAN/auth-mode tests. It explicitly says the new no-login LAN configuration still needs new CI and real-server validation. Spoken Spanish/Catalan small-model accuracy, complete YouTube and GPU acceptance remain pending. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

### Install

The current installer accepts Ubuntu Server 24.04/26.04, prepares Docker/Compose and requires an already assigned private IPv4 address. Replace the README’s illustrative 192.168.5.6 with your own assigned server IP. It configures port 80 and AUTH_REQUIRED=false; optional Basic authentication with HTTPS is a different deployment choice. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

1. Clone the public repository and review the installer/Compose configuration.
2. Assign the intended private IPv4 address in Ubuntu networking first; the installer verifies it but does not configure the network.
3. Replace the source example IP in the command below with that already assigned address, then run the installer as an authorized administrator.
4. Open the server’s private-IP URL from the allowed network and verify the current LAN deployment; do not treat the prior authenticated-container test as its acceptance.

```sh
git clone https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai.git
```


```sh
cd transcribe-ai
```


```sh
sudo bash install.sh 192.168.5.6
```


```sh
sudo docker compose ps
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

1. Open the configured private-server URL from an allowed LAN; current defaults require no account or user SSH tunnel.
2. Upload a short authorized audio/video or a permitted YouTube source and choose automatic, Spanish or Catalan.
3. Review and edit the transcript, particularly silence, noise and language switching.
4. Download the current edited DOCX; paragraphs have no per-phrase timestamps and browser edits do not autosave across reloads.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

- **Hardware:** Owner starting reference: 4 vCPU, 8 GB VM RAM, 25–30 GB expandable disk, not a proven universal minimum. GPU inference/VRAM is not validated. Larger models require separately evaluated resources.
- **Software:** Ubuntu Server 24.04/26.04 installer, Docker Compose v2, CPU small/int8 Faster-Whisper and FFmpeg/FFprobe. Current defaults use assigned private-IP port 80 with login disabled. Compose limits are 4 CPUs/6 GB RAM; models/jobs use separate persistent volumes. The source test report pins av==16.1.0 after a compatibility failure.
- **Platforms:** Linux/Ubuntu inference host with clients accessing its private-IP browser page. Ubuntu 26.04.1 production VM/full Compose acceptance and current no-login LAN deployment remain unverified in the cited report. GPU overlay is explicitly untested; a VirtualBox guest does not automatically receive the host GPU.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** Complete MIT app grant reviewed. Whisper/CTranslate2/model and codec packages, source recordings and optional remote source services retain separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Complete MIT app grant reviewed. Whisper/CTranslate2/model and codec packages, source recordings and optional remote source services retain separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local/server compute and model/job storage; no required commercial transcription API is documented.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md)

### Limitations

No per-user separation is supplied in the no-login LAN default; access depends on the permitted private network and possession of a job ID. It is not a public Internet deployment. The current source says the new LAN mode still needs CI and actual-server validation. DOCX lacks phrase timestamps; bilingual/dialect accuracy is not guaranteed. Initial model download and YouTube require network access, while local-file inference is a separate route. Backups/models persist beyond automatic job cleanup. [Source 1](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/TEST_REPORT.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/install.sh) [Source 4](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/docker-compose.yml)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/christian810/transcribe-ai/blob/main/README.md)

## ScribeRelay

Audio, music & voice · Editing, captions & post-production · Accessible media & assistive creation · Creative publishing & presentation · AI agents for code-authored media production

The repository was created on 2026-11-05. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Create source-timestamped interview transcription artifacts with explicit model reuse and human listening review, optionally on a separately authorized SSH host.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The maintainer explicitly ships v0.1.0 as a technical preview with real speech-recognition, GPU and cross-machine SSH acceptance work still outstanding. The current validation covers synthetic exports rather than that complete intended workflow. https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Current CI uses synthetic text for exports/safety tests. The technical preview explicitly leaves real ASR, GPU compatibility and cross-computer SSH validation pending; synthetic DOCX/SRT output is not a successful spoken-audio test. [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use Python >=3.10, install the package, choose one backend extra and register or explicitly download a matching model. FFmpeg is recommended and required by the separately installed whisper.cpp adapter. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: Release ScribeRelay 0.1.0 technical preview. The current issue/PR endpoint returned no records; this is not evidence of independent support or absence of defects. At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/commit/755454f06d65764dddb96f6dccfcf8cc91c2f311) [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Current documentation, implementation references and the bounded issue/PR sample were examined. Independent successful reproduction of the entire selected workflow remains unverified; this does not assert there are no independent users.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Complete MIT utility grant reviewed. Whisper variants, backend software and downloaded model files retain separate terms; no model weights are redistributed by this utility. Local or trusted remote compute/model storage; no required hosted transcription API is documented. [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. No automatic speaker diarization in v0.1: segments default to Speaker 1 unless source JSON provides labels. Review cues are heuristics, not statistical confidence. Generic export verification cannot compare words with audio or prove word-perfect timing. Remote transfer and model downloads are explicit separate operations.

### How it uses AI

A Python agent-oriented transcription utility selects separately installed Whisper-compatible backends, registers existing model files and exports a structured transcript/subtitle/DOCX review package. [Source](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Python agent-oriented transcription utility selects separately installed Whisper-compatible backends, registers existing model files and exports a structured transcript/subtitle/DOCX review package. [Source 1](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Create source-timestamped interview transcription artifacts with explicit model reuse and human listening review, optionally on a separately authorized SSH host. [Source 1](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

Current CI uses synthetic text for exports/safety tests. The technical preview explicitly leaves real ASR, GPU compatibility and cross-computer SSH validation pending; synthetic DOCX/SRT output is not a successful spoken-audio test. Use Python >=3.10, install the package, choose one backend extra and register or explicitly download a matching model. FFmpeg is recommended and required by the separately installed whisper.cpp adapter. [Source 1](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use Python >=3.10, install the package, choose one backend extra and register or explicitly download a matching model. FFmpeg is recommended and required by the separately installed whisper.cpp adapter.

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/jrspkmi-ai/scriberelay/blob/main/README.md)

## Image to Blender — IDOL mesh workflow

3D, reconstruction & assets · Photogrammetry, scanning & neural rendering · Games & production pipelines · 3D printing & generative CAD

The repository was created on 2026-11-05. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Build an approximate editable prop from an authorized white-background product photo, retaining provenance and the distinction between reconstructed appearance and measured geometry.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-05. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The owner publishes an actual lamp input, uncleaned model-derived mesh and retained run measurements. Visible fragments and unknown back geometry are disclosed. A fresh-process Blender reopen/size check is technical integrity, not visual fidelity or dimensional-accuracy validation. [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/LICENSING.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/BENCHMARKS.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/VALIDATION.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use a separate Python >=3.11 environment for the CLI, obtain the reviewed IDOL SDK revision and compatible CUDA stack/model access, and keep it outside Blender Python. The add-on ZIP for Blender 4.2+ is an independent offline importer, not image inference. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/LICENSING.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/BENCHMARKS.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/VALIDATION.md)
- **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: Publish IDOL-assisted image-to-Blender workflow and offline mesh tools. Latest sampled release: v0.1.0. The current issue/PR endpoint returned no records; this is not evidence of independent support or absence of defects. At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/commit/93b04cd5e4c552cb7e41a86024cc757d4faa9ba7) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/releases/tag/v0.1.0) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Current documentation, implementation references and the bounded issue/PR sample were examined. Independent successful reproduction of the entire selected workflow remains unverified; this does not assert there are no independent users.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Complete GPL-3.0 source grant reviewed; project declares or-later usage. IDOL SDK/checkpoint, DINOv3 custom agreement, BiRefNet, FLUX VAE and example assets remain separate. The maintainer identifies an unresolved exact VAE serialization comparison; do not apply a full-model or component license to every file by assumption. Local GPU/CPU/model storage and separately governed model access; no paid generation API is required by the documented IDOL inference route. [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/LICENSING.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/BENCHMARKS.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/VALIDATION.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. One lamp is not a furniture-quality benchmark. Size controls constrain a bounding box and cannot recover true dimensions or unseen structure; local part scaling can distort joints. Vertex colour is baked appearance, not recovered PBR materials or a directly printable solid. The add-on performs no IDOL/Torch/model download. Source reports local tests while automated CI activation remains unavailable.

### How it uses AI

An independent Python workflow runs IDOL image reconstruction to native Gaussians, extracts a density mesh and exports editable Blender geometry; its separate add-on only imports/resizes already-created mesh packages. [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/LICENSING.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/BENCHMARKS.md) [Source](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/VALIDATION.md)

### Limitations

One lamp is not a furniture-quality benchmark. Size controls constrain a bounding box and cannot recover true dimensions or unseen structure; local part scaling can distort joints. Vertex colour is baked appearance, not recovered PBR materials or a directly printable solid. The add-on performs no IDOL/Torch/model download. Source reports local tests while automated CI activation remains unavailable. [Source 1](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/LICENSING.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/BENCHMARKS.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/docs/VALIDATION.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/dadcolin/image-to-blender/blob/main/README.md)

- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use Python >=3.9 in an isolated environment and install from the cloned source. Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Only MOSS-Image DiT LoRAs are supported. Text-encoder adapters, LoKr/LoHa, damaged numerical weights and changed block-index layouts are not repaired. A loader matching all keys does not prove artistic equivalence.

### How it uses AI

Converts MOSS-Image DiT adapter names and tensor layouts between ComfyUI/kohya/PEFT and mflux, including fused attention/FFN projections that otherwise silently fail to apply a learned visual style. [Source](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Introduction

Converts MOSS-Image DiT adapter names and tensor layouts between ComfyUI/kohya/PEFT and mflux, including fused attention/FFN projections that otherwise silently fail to apply a learned visual style. [Source 1](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### What it is good for

Move an authorized MOSS image-style LoRA between supported training/inference ecosystems and inspect whether its layers actually load. [Source 1](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Demo & examples

Owner comparisons report 246/328 raw keys versus 492/492 converted keys for one comic LoRA and element-wise round trips for two others. These are maintainer observations, not independent style fidelity or arbitrary-model validation. [Source 1](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

### Install

Use Python >=3.9 in an isolated environment and install from the cloned source. The base safetensors backend handles fp16/fp32; bf16 explicitly needs the optional MLX backend. Conversion itself needs no Torch installation. [Source 1](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use Python >=3.9 in an isolated environment and install from the cloned source. The base safetensors backend handles fp16/fp32; bf16 explicitly needs the optional MLX backend. Conversion itself needs no Torch installation.
3. Commands below are source examples for the chosen route, not a cross-platform one-paste installer. Work inside the cloned repository and activate its environment before dependency commands; obtain the documented models/assets first when required.

```sh
python -m pip install -e .
```


```sh
moss-lora-convert my_lora.safetensors --inspect
```


```sh
moss-lora-convert my_lora.safetensors --to mflux
```


```sh
moss-lora-convert my_lora.mflux.safetensors --to comfyui -o converted.safetensors
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

1. Keep the original adapter and inspect format/layer counts.
2. Choose the destination format without overwriting the original.
3. Review warnings: unequal Q/K/V down matrices cannot be fused losslessly and remain separate.
4. Load the converted adapter in a separately prepared inference host and compare a representative authorized prompt/seed.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Gladess1102/moss-lora-convert/blob/main/pyproject.toml)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python >=3.9, setuptools >=68 and safetensors >=0.4; optional mlx >=0.22 for bf16. ComfyUI custom nodes are an alternative integration.
- **Platforms:** The NumPy backend has no documented tested OS matrix; MLX compatibility is separate. No GPU or memory minimum is established for conversion or inference.

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/thanasisgkou/cropus-video-creator/blob/main/README.md)

1. Copy project.example.json to a separate project and set source paths/time zones/work disk/fonts.
2. Ingest, prepare contact sheets/proxies, transcribe and summarize authorized music.
3. Have a separately authorized visual agent or human write watcher notes and an EDL; inspect scenes and speech.
4. Build film.json, run QA, render a 960×540 preview, review it, then request the 1920×1080 master from originals.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/thanasisgkou/cropus-video-creator/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Owner storage estimate is roughly 15–40 GB of proxies/caches for a two-week trip, not a universal minimum. NVIDIA GPU is optional; RAM/VRAM minima are not documented.
- **Software:** Python >=3.10, FFmpeg/ffprobe >=7 (uses -/filter_complex), Pillow/pillow-heif/NumPy/psutil/faster-whisper. An image-reading coding agent is optional; manual JSON editing is supported.
- **Platforms:** Windows/macOS/Linux font discovery is documented. NVIDIA acceleration is optional; CPU libx264/Whisper-int8 fallback is described. Hardware speed and exact cross-platform acceptance remain unverified.

A Windows Streamlit experiment maps BrainFlow EEG band features and ML-derived concentration/mindfulness/relaxation scores into a seeded random-weight neural artwork generator. [Source](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/preprocessing.py) [Source](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/browser_ui.py) [Source](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/generation.py)

### Introduction

A Windows Streamlit experiment maps BrainFlow EEG band features and ML-derived concentration/mindfulness/relaxation scores into a seeded random-weight neural artwork generator. [Source 1](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/preprocessing.py) [Source 3](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/browser_ui.py) [Source 4](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/generation.py)

Explore a small live or synthetic biosignal-driven visual study without claiming the image reveals a person’s thoughts or a measured emotional state. [Source 1](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/preprocessing.py) [Source 3](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/browser_ui.py) [Source 4](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/generation.py)

### Demo & examples

The repository credits the Telepathic Polluck hackathon project. Current source calls BrainFlow MLModel.predict for features; its image generator is a random-weight feed-forward network, not Stable Diffusion or a trained thought-to-image model. [Source 1](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/preprocessing.py) [Source 3](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/browser_ui.py) [Source 4](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt/blob/stable/src/generation.py)

Use the current stable branch, not the minimal main branch. ### How it uses AI

An Apache-2.0 generation core extends TRELLIS.2/Pixal3D for local one- or four-photo textured GLB reconstruction, with staged FP8 loading, camera estimation and optional photograph-pixel texture projection. [Source](https://github.com/Quentincls/localmesh-engine/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Quentincls/localmesh-engine/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source](https://github.com/Quentincls/localmesh-engine/blob/main/docs/WEIGHTS.md) [Source](https://github.com/Quentincls/localmesh-engine/blob/main/NOTICE)

- **Platforms:** Documented references include Ubuntu 18.04 x86/aarch64 NVIDIA and Ubuntu 22.04 CPU in a Windows-hosted VM. RK/Ascend/MLU tests are listed but some associated code is not provided; “any platform” is not a tested Mac/Windows-native matrix.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/sherlockchou86/VideoPipe/blob/master/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/sherlockchou86/VideoPipe/blob/master/README.md)

- **Code:** Apache-2.0
- **Weights:** Complete Apache-2.0 root grant reviewed. Independent third_party projects, detection/generation models, codecs and optional services retain separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows Apache-2.0. Complete Apache-2.0 root grant reviewed. Independent third_party projects, detection/generation models, codecs and optional services retain separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local video/model compute and optional selected service/hardware; no required cloud inference for the CPU baseline.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/sherlockchou86/VideoPipe/blob/master/README.md)

### Limitations

This is an integration framework, not its own trained model. Most examples need source adaptation, model/output parsing and explicit data paths. No general RAM/VRAM requirement or real-time latency bound is established. Model recognition, face swap and tracking require route-specific rights/accuracy review. The current thread/queue race discussion remains unresolved in the sampled support record; the review did not establish production-safe concurrent processing. [Source 1](https://github.com/sherlockchou86/VideoPipe/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/sherlockchou86/VideoPipe/issues/84)

Download DWPose/MimicMotion weights separately and authorize the SVD model’s terms before its automatic download. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. - **Maintenance and support · documented:** Latest sampled commit: [compat] add compatibility for low-version torch. Current bounded support sample: Apple Silicon (MPS) support + pipeline fixes and small perf wins (closed); Running  (open); Add tokenizer (closed). At most five releases, ten commits and fifteen recently updated issue/PR records were captured. This is a bounded source review, not an exhaustive blocker audit or a support-quality pass. [Source](https://github.com/Tencent/MimicMotion/commit/6907bdcc259a6a048d41a365e840d22274f9256c) [Source](https://github.com/Tencent/MimicMotion/pull/122) [Source](https://github.com/Tencent/MimicMotion/issues/124) [Source](https://github.com/Tencent/MimicMotion/pull/123)

Local model/GPU resources and separately governed checkpoint access; no hosted generation API is required. Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Owner reference: a 35-second demo uses a 72-frame model/16 GB VRAM and about 20 minutes on RTX4090. The 16-frame U-Net can fit 8 GB but VAE wants 16 GB unless moved to CPU. Those stage observations are not a universal speed or RAM/storage minimum.

### How it uses AI

An Apache-2.0 inference framework uses confidence-aware pose guidance and a video-diffusion backbone to animate a reference person according to an authorized motion video. [Source](https://github.com/Tencent/MimicMotion/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Tencent/MimicMotion/blob/main/environment.yaml)

### Introduction

An Apache-2.0 inference framework uses confidence-aware pose guidance and a video-diffusion backbone to animate a reference person according to an authorized motion video. ### How it uses AI

A GPL symbolic co-creative music toolkit learns probabilistic melody/chord continuations from MusicXML examples and combines them with human-crafted structure/variation rules; it does not synthesize finished audio.
