- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Complete MIT app grant reviewed. Meta model/API, Google optional sign-in, hosting/storage, source conversations and generated image rights are separate; self-hosting the app does not self-host these four models.
- **Cost:** Chosen Meta model/API access and server resources; mock-mode tests use no credits and do not establish a free production entitlement.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/NamiduHarshana/minutecanvas/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NamiduHarshana/minutecanvas/blob/main/backend/README.md)

### Limitations

A matched transcript quote proves it came from the ASR text, not that the text or inferred conflict is true. Audio is intended not to persist, but provider handling and debug logging are separate. Replays keep transcript/images for a configurable period (default 30 days, pinned demos excepted); backend restart ends active sessions. [Source 1](https://github.com/NamiduHarshana/minutecanvas/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NamiduHarshana/minutecanvas/blob/main/backend/README.md)

### Install

Install the plugin in a compatible Claude Code client, restart and run its one-time setup. It checks Node/FFmpeg/Python, prepares a private Python environment and Remotion workspace, and separately signs in to Fish Audio if requested. [Source 1](https://github.com/triftan/video-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/triftan/video-studio/blob/main/plugins/video-studio/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the plugin in a compatible Claude Code client, restart and run its one-time setup. It checks Node/FFmpeg/Python, prepares a private Python environment and Remotion workspace, and separately signs in to Fish Audio if requested.
3. Commands below are source examples for the chosen route, not a cross-platform one-paste installer. Work inside the cloned repository and activate its environment before dependency commands; obtain the documented models/assets first when required.

```sh
/plugin marketplace add triftan/video-studio
```


```sh
/plugin install video-studio@video-studio
```


```sh
/video-studio:setup
```


```sh
/video-studio:catalog
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/triftan/video-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/triftan/video-studio/blob/main/plugins/video-studio/README.md)

1. Review proposed tool installation/settings and choose a separate workspace.
2. Select a style preview and supply authorized media/brief.
3. Choose Fish/another authorized voice, a separately installed local Kokoro route or your own recording.
4. Inspect motion, captions and actual sound before final export; font substitutions can change layout.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/triftan/video-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/triftan/video-studio/blob/main/plugins/video-studio/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Child guide suggests about 1 GB free disk for its initial workspace, not a complete dependency/model/media storage minimum. RAM/VRAM minima are not documented.
- **Software:** Compatible Claude Code/agent access, Node, FFmpeg, Python/private environment and Remotion. Exact pinned tool/runtime versions need the setup references; no universal numeric RAM/VRAM requirement is documented.
- **Platforms:** Setup has macOS/Linux shell and Windows PowerShell routes. Platform prompts/dependencies can differ; the documentation is not a measured all-platform performance matrix.

### Sahne / Backstage · MIT

Agent-assisted writing and human review of interactive illustrated dialogue scenes with editable story documents, lip sync and code-drawn stages.
The implemented Backstage assistant uses a LangChain/LangGraph model/tool loop and MCP story/kit tools to author and revise dialogue, characters and staging for the deterministic paper-theatre engine.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Scope is the MIT engine/editor code and explicitly licensed non-reserved resources. The full art notice separately grants most kit art CC BY 4.0 but reserves Kerim and named cast parts; those assets are not licensed for general reuse. Node 24/pnpm 11 are documented, while local model/Anthropic/OpenAI-compatible service setup, budgets and privacy require a deeper workflow review. Browser/Node tests and owner gallery pictures do not independently verify generated-story quality.

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **OpenDesign** · excluded: The current full LICENSE changes operative wording in standard Apache 2.0 sections 6 and 9. The modified/custom grant is not verified as unmodified Apache-2.0 despite the badge; eligibility is held pending clarification. [Source](https://github.com/nexu-io/open-design/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **MagicDance / MagicPose** · excluded: The complete current USC grant limits code to educational, research and nonprofit use. That restricted software grant does not meet this library’s open-source-only policy. [Source](https://github.com/Boese0601/MagicDance)
- **Bailando** · excluded: The complete current S-Lab software grant includes non-commercial conditions, so the code is excluded under the open-source-only policy. [Source](https://github.com/lisiyao21/Bailando)
- **UniAnimate** · needs-license-review: The current repository license endpoint returned 404; a complete software grant was not established. This is an evidence gap, not proof that no license exists anywhere. [Source](https://github.com/ali-vilab/UniAnimate)
- **ComicFlow Validator** · excluded: The inspected current implementation is a bootstrap/validation scaffold; its proposed creative AI validation workflow was not established as implemented. A name or roadmap is insufficient creative AI evidence. [Source](https://github.com/a18714466979-cmd/ComicFlow-Validator)
- **Auteur** · excluded: The current implementation primarily records browser demonstrations while the README places AI captioning/director/render behavior in progress. An implemented qualifying creative AI route was not established. [Source](https://github.com/ali-maruf/auteur)
- **Museum Semantic Search** · needs-license-review: The README claims MIT but the complete repository license endpoint returned 404. The current demo URL is not treated as a full software grant. [Source](https://github.com/derekphilipau/museum-semantic-search)
- **Voronoi Stained Glass** · needs-license-review: The complete license endpoint returned 404, and a qualifying implemented creative AI role was not established beyond conventional optimization. [Source](https://github.com/dhudsmith/voronoi-stained-glass)
- **EmbroideryForge** · needs-license-review: The current source describes a DeepSeek-assisted image-to-stitch-plan/DST pipeline, but the full repository license endpoint returned 404. No complete open-source software grant was established. [Source](https://github.com/EXPFAL/Embroideryforge)
- **Deep WaveNet Underwater Image Restoration** · needs-license-review: The current repository request returned 404. Source and complete software terms could not be verified; this does not establish abandonment or present licensing elsewhere. [Source](https://github.com/pksvision/Deep-WaveNet-UnderwaterImage-Restoration)
